Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The energy operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 902. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 532 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the energy space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 902 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 532. QED.
